########################################
# Cosmo General Events
#
# 
########################################

namespace = cosmo_fakenw_events


#First Menu madams
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_fakenw_events.1001
	title = cosmo_fakenw_events.1001.t
	desc = cosmo_fakenw_events.1001.d
	picture = cosmogfx_BROTHEL_FAKE_NEW_WORLD_MENU_1_eventPicture

	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		NOT = {
			OR = {
			has_country_flag = brothel_fake_newworld_menu_all
			has_country_flag = brothel_fake_newworld_menu_1	
			any_known_country = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = brothel_fake_newworld_menu_all
				has_country_flag = brothel_fake_newworld_menu_1
			}	
			}
			}		
		}
		OR = {
		any_owned_province = {
			region = carribeans_region
		}
		any_known_country = {
			any_owned_province = {
			has_building = brothel_district
			}
			NOT = {
						culture_group = andean_group		
						culture_group = aridoamerican		
						culture_group = otomanguean		
						culture_group = je_tupi		
						culture_group = je		
						culture_group = maranon		
						culture_group = chibchan		
						culture_group = carribean
						culture_group = maya		
						culture_group = central_american		
						culture_group = mataco		
						culture_group = araucanian
						culture_group = eskaleut		
						culture_group = central_algonquian		
						culture_group = plains_algonquian		
						culture_group = eastern_algonquian		
						culture_group = iroquoian		
						culture_group = siouan		
						culture_group = caddoan		
						culture_group = muskogean
						culture_group = sonoran		
						culture_group = apachean		
						culture_group = na_dene		
						culture_group = penutian
			}			
		any_owned_province = {
			region = carribeans_region
			has_discovered = ROOT
			}
		}			
		}
			any_owned_province = {
				has_building = brothel_district
			}
		any_province = {
			region = carribeans_region
			range = ROOT
		}
			NOT = {
						culture_group = andean_group		
						culture_group = aridoamerican		
						culture_group = otomanguean		
						culture_group = je_tupi		
						culture_group = je		
						culture_group = maranon		
						culture_group = chibchan		
						culture_group = carribean
						culture_group = maya		
						culture_group = central_american		
						culture_group = mataco		
						culture_group = araucanian
						culture_group = eskaleut		
						culture_group = central_algonquian		
						culture_group = plains_algonquian		
						culture_group = eastern_algonquian		
						culture_group = iroquoian		
						culture_group = siouan		
						culture_group = caddoan		
						culture_group = muskogean
						culture_group = sonoran		
						culture_group = apachean		
						culture_group = na_dene		
						culture_group = penutian
			}		
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 60
	}	

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {

					any_province = {
					region = carribeans_region
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					}		
			}
			set_country_flag = brothel_fake_newworld_menu_1
            set_country_flag = madams_estate_active
			}			
			}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_fakenw_events.1001.a
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = brothel_fake_newworld_menu_1
			}
		country_event = {
			id = cosmo_fakenw_events.1002
			}			
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = brothel_fake_newworld_menu_1
			}	
			}
		country_event = {
			id = cosmo_fakenw_events.1003
			}			
		}												
	}	
}

#First Menu madams
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_fakenw_events.1002
	title = cosmo_fakenw_events.1002.t
	desc = cosmo_fakenw_events.1002.d
	picture = cosmogfx_BROTHEL_FAKE_NEW_WORLD_MENU_1_eventPicture

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
	}


	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
				set_country_flag = brothel_fake_newworld_menu_1	
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
					region = carribeans_region
				}
					save_event_target_as = colonyprovince
				}						
			}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_fakenw_events.1002.a
		random_province = {
			limit = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 			
			has_building = brothel_district
			}
		add_province_modifier = {
			name = fake_south_american_prostitutes
			duration = 36500  
			desc = fake_south_american_prostitutes_DESC

		}
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_madams
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_PROSTITUTES_NEW_WORLD
			influence = -5
			duration = 9125
		}
		add_estate_loyalty = {
			estate = estate_madams
			loyalty = 5
		}										
	}	
}


#First Menu madams
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_fakenw_events.1003
	title = cosmo_fakenw_events.1003.t
	desc = cosmo_fakenw_events.1003.d
	picture = cosmogfx_BROTHEL_FAKE_NEW_WORLD_MENU_1_eventPicture

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
	}


	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
				random_known_country = {
				limit = {
				any_owned_province = {
					has_building = brothel_district
				}
				any_owned_province = {
					region = carribeans_region
				}
				}				
					set_country_flag = brothel_fake_newworld_menu_1
					save_event_target_as = prostitutecountry
				}			
			}
	}	

	option = {
		name = cosmo_fakenw_events.1003.a
												
	}

	option = {
		name = cosmo_fakenw_events.1003.b
			event_target:prostitutecountry = {
				add_spy_network_in = {
					who = ROOT
					value = 20
				}
			}
			random_province = {
				limit = {
					owner = {
					has_country_flag = brothel_fake_newworld_menu_1
					}
					is_capital = yes 
				}
				add_scaled_local_dip_power = -25
			}
			random_province = {
			limit = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 			
			has_building = brothel_district
			}
		add_province_modifier = {
			name = fake_south_american_prostitutes
			duration = 36500  
			desc = fake_south_american_prostitutes_DESC

		}
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_madams
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_PROSTITUTES_NEW_WORLD
			influence = -5
			duration = 9125
		}
		add_estate_loyalty = {
			estate = estate_madams
			loyalty = 5
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			set_country_flag = brothel_fake_newworld_menu_1
			event_target:prostitutecountry = {
			#clr_country_flag = brothel_fake_newworld_menu_1
			}
		}											
	}	
}

			limit = {

			}
		country_event = {
			id = cosmo_fakenw_events.2002
			}			
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = brothel_fake_newworld_menu_2
			}	
			}
		country_event = {
			id = cosmo_fakenw_events.2003
			}

country_event = {
	id = cosmo_fakenw_events.3001
	title = cosmo_fakenw_events.3001.t
	desc = cosmo_fakenw_events.3001.d
	picture = cosmogfx_BROTHEL_FAKE_NEW_WORLD_MENU_3_eventPicture

	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		NOT = {
			OR = {
			has_country_flag = brothel_fake_newworld_menu_all
			has_country_flag = brothel_fake_newworld_menu_3

			OR = {
				has_country_flag = brothel_fake_newworld_menu_all
				has_country_flag = brothel_fake_newworld_menu_3
			}	
			}
			}		
		}
		OR = {
		any_owned_province = {
				OR = {
					region = mexico_region
					region = central_america_region
				}
		}
		any_known_country = {
			any_owned_province = {
			has_building = brothel_district
			}

						culture_group = iroquoian		
						culture_group = siouan

						culture_group = sonoran

		days = 60
	}

#Fourth Menu madams
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_fakenw_events.4001
	title = cosmo_fakenw_events.4001.t
	desc = cosmo_fakenw_events.4001.d
	picture = cosmogfx_BROTHEL_FAKE_NEW_WORLD_MENU_4_eventPicture

	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		NOT = {
			OR = {
			has_country_flag = brothel_fake_newworld_menu_all
			has_country_flag = brothel_fake_newworld_menu_4	
			any_known_country = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = brothel_fake_newworld_menu_all
				has_country_flag = brothel_fake_newworld_menu_4
			}	
			}
			}		
		}
		OR = {
		any_owned_province = {
		OR = {
			region = california_region
			region = rio_grande_region
		}	
		}
		any_known_country = {
			any_owned_province = {
			has_building = brothel_district
			}
			NOT = {
						culture_group = andean_group		
						culture_group = aridoamerican		
						culture_group = otomanguean		
						culture_group = je_tupi		
						culture_group = je		
						culture_group = maranon		
						culture_group = chibchan		
						culture_group = carribean
						culture_group = maya		
						culture_group = central_american		
						culture_group = mataco		
						culture_group = araucanian
						culture_group = eskaleut		
						culture_group = central_algonquian		
						culture_group = plains_algonquian		
						culture_group = eastern_algonquian		
						culture_group = iroquoian		
						culture_group = siouan		
						culture_group = caddoan		
						culture_group = muskogean
						culture_group = sonoran		
						culture_group = apachean		
						culture_group = na_dene		
						culture_group = penutian
			}			
		any_owned_province = {
		OR = {
			region = california_region
			region = rio_grande_region
		}
			has_discovered = ROOT
			}
		}			
		}
			any_owned_province = {
				has_building = brothel_district
			}
		any_province = {
		OR = {
			region = california_region
			region = rio_grande_region
		}
			range = ROOT
		}
			NOT = {
						culture_group = andean_group		
						culture_group = aridoamerican		
						culture_group = otomanguean		
						culture_group = je_tupi		
						culture_group = je		
						culture_group = maranon		
						culture_group = chibchan		
						culture_group = carribean
						culture_group = maya		
						culture_group = central_american		
						culture_group = mataco		
						culture_group = araucanian
						culture_group = eskaleut		
						culture_group = central_algonquian		
						culture_group = plains_algonquian		
						culture_group = eastern_algonquian		
						culture_group = iroquoian		
						culture_group = siouan		
						culture_group = caddoan		
						culture_group = muskogean
						culture_group = sonoran		
						culture_group = apachean		
						culture_group = na_dene		
						culture_group = penutian
			}		
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 60
	}	

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {

					any_province = {
				OR = {
			region = california_region
			region = rio_grande_region
		}
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					}		
			}
			set_country_flag = brothel_fake_newworld_menu_4
            set_country_flag = madams_estate_active
			}			
			}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_fakenw_events.4001.a
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = brothel_fake_newworld_menu_4
			}
		country_event = {
			id = cosmo_fakenw_events.4002
			}			
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = brothel_fake_newworld_menu_4
			}	
			}
		country_event = {
			id = cosmo_fakenw_events.4003
			}			
		}												
	}	
}

#Fourth Menu madams
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_fakenw_events.4002
	title = cosmo_fakenw_events.4002.t
	desc = cosmo_fakenw_events.4002.d
	picture = cosmogfx_BROTHEL_FAKE_NEW_WORLD_MENU_4_eventPicture

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
	}


	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
				set_country_flag = brothel_fake_newworld_menu_4			
			}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_fakenw_events.4002.a
		random_province = {
			limit = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 			
			has_building = brothel_district
			}
		add_province_modifier = {
			name = fake_north_american_prostitutes
			duration = 36500  
			desc = fake_north_american_prostitutes_DESC

		}
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_madams
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_PROSTITUTES_NEW_WORLD
			influence = -5
			duration = 9125
		}
		add_estate_loyalty = {
			estate = estate_madams
			loyalty = 5
		}										
	}	
}


#Fourth Menu madams
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_fakenw_events.4003
	title = cosmo_fakenw_events.4003.t
	desc = cosmo_fakenw_events.4003.d
	picture = cosmogfx_BROTHEL_FAKE_NEW_WORLD_MENU_4_eventPicture

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
	}


	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
				random_known_country = {
				limit = {
				any_owned_province = {
					has_building = brothel_district
				}
				any_owned_province = {
				OR = {
			region = california_region
			region = rio_grande_region
		}
				}
				}				
					set_country_flag = brothel_fake_newworld_menu_4
					save_event_target_as = prostitutecountry
				}			
			}
	}	

	option = {
		name = cosmo_fakenw_events.4003.a
												
	}

	option = {
		name = cosmo_fakenw_events.4003.b
			event_target:prostitutecountry = {
				add_spy_network_in = {
					who = ROOT
					value = 20
				}
			}
			random_province = {
				limit = {
					owner = {
					has_country_flag = brothel_fake_newworld_menu_4
					}
					is_capital = yes 
				}
				add_scaled_local_dip_power = -25
			}
			random_province = {
			limit = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 			
			has_building = brothel_district
			}
		add_province_modifier = {
			name = fake_north_american_prostitutes
			duration = 36500  
			desc = fake_north_american_prostitutes_DESC

		}
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_madams
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_PROSTITUTES_NEW_WORLD
			influence = -5
			duration = 9125
		}
		add_estate_loyalty = {
			estate = estate_madams
			loyalty = 5
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			set_country_flag = brothel_fake_newworld_menu_4
			event_target:prostitutecountry = {
			#clr_country_flag = brothel_fake_newworld_menu_4
			}
		}											
	}	
}

country_event = {
	id = cosmo_fakenw_events.7001
	title = cosmo_fakenw_events.7001.t
	desc = cosmo_fakenw_events.7001.d
	picture = cosmogfx_BROTHEL_FAKE_NEW_WORLD_MENU_7_eventPicture

	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		NOT = {
			OR = {
			has_country_flag = brothel_fake_newworld_menu_all
			has_country_flag = brothel_fake_newworld_menu_7	
			any_known_country = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = brothel_fake_newworld_menu_all
				has_country_flag = brothel_fake_newworld_menu_7
			}	
			}
			}		
		}
		OR = {
		any_owned_province = {
		OR = {
			region = great_plains_region
		}	
		}
		any_known_country = {
			any_owned_province = {
			has_building = brothel_district
			}
			NOT = {
						culture_group = andean_group		
						culture_group = aridoamerican		
						culture_group = otomanguean		
						culture_group = je_tupi		
						culture_group = je		
						culture_group = maranon		
						culture_group = chibchan		
						culture_group = carribean
						culture_group = maya		
						culture_group = central_american		
						culture_group = mataco		
						culture_group = araucanian
						culture_group = eskaleut		
						culture_group = central_algonquian		
						culture_group = plains_algonquian		
						culture_group = eastern_algonquian		
						culture_group = iroquoian		
						culture_group = siouan		
						culture_group = caddoan		
						culture_group = muskogean
						culture_group = sonoran		
						culture_group = apachean		
						culture_group = na_dene		
						culture_group = penutian
			}			
		any_owned_province = {
		OR = {
			region = great_plains_region
		}
			has_discovered = ROOT
			}
		}			
		}
			any_owned_province = {
				has_building = brothel_district
			}
		any_province = {
		OR = {
			region = great_plains_region
		}
			range = ROOT
		}
			NOT = {
						culture_group = andean_group		
						culture_group = aridoamerican		
						culture_group = otomanguean		
						culture_group = je_tupi		
						culture_group = je		
						culture_group = maranon		
						culture_group = chibchan		
						culture_group = carribean
						culture_group = maya		
						culture_group = central_american		
						culture_group = mataco		
						culture_group = araucanian
						culture_group = eskaleut		
						culture_group = central_algonquian		
						culture_group = plains_algonquian		
						culture_group = eastern_algonquian		
						culture_group = iroquoian		
						culture_group = siouan		
						culture_group = caddoan		
						culture_group = muskogean
						culture_group = sonoran		
						culture_group = apachean		
						culture_group = na_dene		
						culture_group = penutian
			}		
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 60
	}	

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {

					any_province = {
				OR = {
			region = great_plains_region
		}
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					}		
			}
			set_country_flag = brothel_fake_newworld_menu_7
            set_country_flag = madams_estate_active
			}			
			}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_fakenw_events.7001.a
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = brothel_fake_newworld_menu_7
			}
		country_event = {
			id = cosmo_fakenw_events.7002
			}			
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = brothel_fake_newworld_menu_7
			}	
			}
		country_event = {
			id = cosmo_fakenw_events.7003
			}			
		}												
	}

	}

			}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_fakenw_events.7002.a
		random_province = {
			limit = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 			
			has_building = brothel_district
			}
		add_province_modifier = {
			name = fake_north_american_prostitutes
			duration = 36500  
			desc = fake_north_american_prostitutes_DESC

		}
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_madams
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_PROSTITUTES_NEW_WORLD
			influence = -5
			duration = 9125
		}
		add_estate_loyalty = {
			estate = estate_madams
			loyalty = 5
		}										
	}

country_event = {
	id = cosmo_fakenw_events.7003
	title = cosmo_fakenw_events.7003.t
	desc = cosmo_fakenw_events.7003.d
	picture = cosmogfx_BROTHEL_FAKE_NEW_WORLD_MENU_7_eventPicture

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
	}


	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
				random_known_country = {
				limit = {
				any_owned_province = {
					has_building = brothel_district
				}
				any_owned_province = {
				OR = {
			region = great_plains_region
		}
				}
				}				
					set_country_flag = brothel_fake_newworld_menu_7
					save_event_target_as = prostitutecountry
				}			
			}
	}	

	option = {
		name = cosmo_fakenw_events.7003.a
												
	}

	option = {
		name = cosmo_fakenw_events.7003.b
			event_target:prostitutecountry = {
				add_spy_network_in = {
					who = ROOT
					value = 20
				}
			}
			random_province = {
				limit = {
					owner = {
					has_country_flag = brothel_fake_newworld_menu_7
					}
					is_capital = yes 
				}
				add_scaled_local_dip_power = -25
			}
			random_province = {
			limit = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 			
			has_building = brothel_district
			}
		add_province_modifier = {
			name = fake_north_american_prostitutes
			duration = 36500  
			desc = fake_north_american_prostitutes_DESC

		}
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_madams
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_PROSTITUTES_NEW_WORLD
			influence = -5
			duration = 9125
		}
		add_estate_loyalty = {
			estate = estate_madams
			loyalty = 5
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			set_country_flag = brothel_fake_newworld_menu_7
			event_target:prostitutecountry = {
			#clr_country_flag = brothel_fake_newworld_menu_7
			}
		}											
	}	
}
